# Architecture specs of all neural networks (dts_solution)

Auto-generated by `scripts/arch_specs.py` + `scripts/arch_specs_md.py` (CPU, weights loaded from the listed checkpoints, param counts from torch). Machine-readable: `docs/ARCH_SPECS.json` (full module trees inside).

## Conventions
- Grid: dx 0.25 m (X axis), dt 120 s (T axis); tensors are (B, C, T, X); tile 128x256 train, 256x512 overlap 32 infer.
- Input 3 channels: ch0 dT_time (T - common-mode(t) - static profile(x)) / sigma(x); ch1 dT_space (T - rolling median over x 40 m - common-mode) / sigma(x); ch2 valid mask.
- Shapes below are for a dummy tile 1x3x128x256 (T=128 rows = 4.3 h, X=256 cols = 64 m); all segmentation nets are fully convolutional.
- PlotNeuralNet: per-layer table gives name, type, in/out ch, out shape (B,C,T,X), kernel, stride, dil, pad; skip links (link to) are listed per model under Skip connections.

## Ensemble / pipeline diagram
```
  RELEASE ens_gen2 (docs/RELEASE_DECISION.md); API dts.infer.release_analyzer() (gen2='on'); rollback gen2='off' = ens_u2ft_tcn_lgbm
  raw DTS T(t,x) -> regrid 0.25 m x 120 s -> make_channels -> X (3, T, X)
  winner members (parallel): unet_w24 [det0], unet_v810ft w16 [det1], tcn w32 [det2], pixel_lgbm [det3, LightGBM on per-pixel features, not NN]
  each -> p_anom = 1 - softmax(bg) (3 classes bg/heat/cool), tiled 256x512 overlap 32
  s_winner = mean of 4 score maps (ens_u2ft_tcn_lgbm)
  GEN2 U-Net w16 [models_release_gen2/s3_gen2_unet.pt] -> s_gen2 = 1 - softmax(bg)
  score = 0.5 * (s_winner + s_gen2); hotness = 0.5 * (h_winner + h_gen2)
  threshold at FAR 1e-3 on full v8 val background (all val sequences, synthetic): thr = 0.8580 (old release ens_u2ft_tcn_lgbm: 0.8608)
  connected components (zones=cc) -> zones; static=off, mae=off (not calibrated together with gen2)
  stage 2: EventNet CNN(32x64 crop)+MLP(scalars) blended 0.5 with LightGBM -> 7 event types
```

```
X(3,T,X) --+--> unet_w24 ----\
           +--> unet_v810ft --+--> mean --> thr@FAR1e-3 (0.861) --> CC / PDRS zones (+STATIC opt.) --> stage2 EventNet+LGBM --> 7 types
           +--> tcn ----------/
           +--> pixel_lgbm --/   (box, not NN)
```

## 7. MAE masked reconstructor (ens_mean4 member)

- Status: CANDIDATE (flag mae=on, not default): ens_mean4 val 0.3995/test 0.4336/v10 0.4299 vs winner 0.3867/0.4053/0.4039; same-fold nested +0.0068 (k5) +0.0100 (k2). NOTE: checkpoint = INIT weights: best_epoch=-1 means no epoch beat the epoch-0 (pre-training) proxy, so the saved state is the initialisation; head is zero-initialised (hat=0), so the MAE score reduces exactly to CDF(|dT_space|) and ens_mean4 = (4*winner + CDF|dT_space|)/5, a fixed post-filter (artifacts/eval/s3_mae_why.json, docs/S3_MAE.md); trained seeds unstable (-0.031..+0.014); stays flag mae=on, not default
- Checkpoint: loaded artifacts/eval/s3_mae_recon.pt (strict)
- Params: 24,130 total, 24,130 trainable; layer-table sum 24,130
- Input: ch (2: dT_time,dT_space) masked by spatial bands; valid; mask (1=hidden). Anomaly score = |recon residual| on hidden cells, 4 mask phases; shapes [[1, 2, 128, 256], [1, 1, 128, 256], [1, 1, 128, 256]]
- Output: [1, 2, 128, 256]; head: Conv1x1 -> 2 (reconstructed dT_time, dT_space)
- Data flow: in = cat[ch*vis, vis, mask] (4ch) -> e1(5x3,3x3) -> e2(s2) -> e3(s2) -> up2 +e2 -> d2 -> up1 +e1 -> d1 -> ctx(dil2,dil4) -> head 1x1 -> 2ch recon * valid
- Skip connections: e1 + up1(d2) -> d1 (additive); e2 + up2(e3) -> d2 (additive)
- Note: ens_mean4 = (4 x ens_u2ft_tcn_lgbm score + MAE residual score)/5, fixed a priori; threshold FAR 1e-3 on v8 val background -> see S3_MAE.md

- Status: RELEASE member of ens_gen2 (=mean(gen2, ens_u2ft_tcn_lgbm); val 0.4106 / test 0.4579 / v10 0.4576 vs 0.3867/0.4053/0.4039; held-out41 +0.018 (2 seeds); nested k5 +0.031 on all 45 v8 val seq (incl. 4 checkpoint-selection proxy files) vs +0.051 on the 41 held-out seq (clean, used for the decision); thr 0.8580; see RELEASE_DECISION.md)
- Checkpoint: loaded models_release_gen2/s3_gen2_unet.pt (strict)
- Params: 571,539 total, 571,539 trainable; layer-table sum 571,539
- Input: ch0 dT_time (T - common-mode(t) - static profile(x)) / sigma(x); ch1 dT_space (T - rolling median over x 40 m - common-mode) / sigma(x); ch2 valid mask; shapes [[1, 3, 128, 256]]
- Output: [1, 3, 128, 256]; head: Conv1x1 -> 3 logits
- Data flow: e1(k7x3) -mp2-> e2(k7x3) -mp2-> e3 -mp2-> e4 -> TimeGate -> u3(convT2) ++e3 -> d3 -> u2 ++e2 -> d2 -> u1 ++e1 -> d1 -> head 1x1 (3 cls: bg/heat/cool), p_anom = 1 - softmax_bg; finetuned from unet_v810ft on synth v8+v10 train with on-the-fly dts/gen2.py: narrow static hot spots 2-8 m +3..+24C, broad slow profile shifts, HVAC cycles 20-90 min, instrument -20C dips as negative class; BN frozen
- Skip connections: e1 -> cat -> d1; e2 -> cat -> d2; e3 -> cat -> d3; e4 -> TimeGate -> u3
